## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，我开个任务去做，弄清楚了告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can track down and fix a failing test in one of your repositories.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트 고쳐줘"나 "내일 아침 9시에 오늘 메일 요약해줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 바로 맡아서 처리할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```

  [partner message] 안녕히 계세요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先して届けっぱなしにする通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認し、欠けた分を再送し、順番も揃えて渡します。UDPはそうした確認をせず、送ったデータをそのまま送るので、遅延が小さく、動画配信やオンラインゲームなどに向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu propia rama antes de compartirla, porque deja el historial lineal y limpio. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo se integró el trabajo, porque nunca debes reescribir commits que otros ya tienen.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、按顺序交付的；UDP 则不保证送达和顺序，但没有建连和重传开销，传输更快更轻。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you're tidying your own unshared branch before it goes into the main line, so the history reads as one clean story. Merge when the branch is shared or already public, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have based work on.
```

  [opened a task] title: Bun 1.4 Linux bun build --compile 踩坑整理 | place: {"kind":"browser","profile":"default","device":"local"} | runs on computer: the home (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："帮我查一下 Bun 1.4 在 Linux 上用 bun build --compile 有哪些坑，整理成一页文档" ⏎  ⏎ 语言：简体中文。 ⏎  ⏎ 要做的事： ⏎ 1. 查 Bun 1.4 官方文档、Bun 发行说明（1.4.x 各版本的 changelog）、GitHub issues（oven-sh/bun）中关于 Linux 上 `bun build --compile` 的已知问题、坑和绕过办法。 ⏎ 2. 重点覆盖：glibc 与 musl（Alpine）差异、交叉编译目标（--target，如 bun-linux-x64 / bun-linux-arm64 / -baseline / -musl 变体）、动态链接与 libc 依赖、体积与启动时间、运行时读取文件（import.meta.dir、__dirname、process.execPath、静态资源嵌入 with `with { type: "file" }`）、node_modules 中原生addon 的打包、环境变量与 .env 的行为、以及 Bun 1.4 相对之前版本的变化。 ⏎ 3. 每一条坑写清：现象、影响的版本、是否已修复（写明修复版本号）、绕过办法。查不到的别编，写"未查到"。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 输出一页的中文文档（约 600 字以内，Markdown），按"坑 → 现象 → 绕过办法 → 来源链接"列出。 ⏎ - 每条都附来源链接（官方文档、changelog 或 issue 地址）。 ⏎ - 说明查阅的时间，以及哪些结论只是来自 issue 讨论、未在本机验证。 ⏎  ⏎ 不在范围内： ⏎ - 不在本机实际编译或运行任何东西，不改动任何仓库或文件。 ⏎ - 不登录任何网站，不发帖、不评论 issue。 ⏎ - 不要把查不到的内容凭记忆补上。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：只做网页查阅（浏览器），结果直接放在报告里，不需要写文件。

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it's applied to the main data, so a crash mid-write can be replayed or rolled back instead of corrupting the database. It trades a little extra write work for durability and fast recovery.
```

(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with nights 2 and 3 filled in, and each new benchmark run will add itself.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1708 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes per second and p99 latency, every night at 23:00</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Nothing yet: the first night's result lands here.</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return n == null ? '—' : (n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n/1000) + 'k' : String(n)); }
function render(v){
  var runs = ((v && v.runs) || []).slice().sort(function(a,b){ return b.night - a.night; });
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!runs.length) { empty.style.display = ''; return; }
  empty.style.display = 'none';
  var top = runs[0];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(top.writes) + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'Night ' + top.night + ' · p99 ' + top.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + (top.stalls == null ? '—' : top.stalls);
  runs.forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">Night ' + r.night + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + (r.stalls == null ? '—' : r.stalls) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(r.writes) + '/s</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":{"runs":[{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0},{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null}]}}

    <div class="k-field">
      <label>哪天</label>
      <div class="k-chips" id="dayChips"></div>
      <input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/24">

(function () {
  var items = [];
  var sel = null;

      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function money(n) { return '¥' + n.toFixed(2); }

  function render() {
    var total = items.reduce(function (s, i) {  … );
    var amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
    if (!day || !what || !(amt > 0)) return;
    items.push({
      id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
      day: day,
      what: what,
      amount: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100
    });
    save();
    document.getElementById('what').value = '';
    document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
    render();
  });

  document.getElementById('dayChips').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('[data-day]');
    if (b) document.getElementById('day').value = b.getAttribute('data-day');
  });

  document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
      items = items.filter(function (i) { return i.id !== id; });
      sel = null;
      save();
      render();
      return;
    }
    var row = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
    if (row) {
      var id2 = row.getAttribute('data-id');
      sel = sel === id2 ? null : id2;
      render();
    }
  });

  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(total):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");
    wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, and you tick each one off as it lands.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2458 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <span class="k-sum-label">Not landed yet</span>
    <span class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</span>
    <span class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tick each one as it lands</span>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { cursor: pointer; }
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-tick { display:inline-block; width:20px; height:20px; border-radius:6px; border:2px solid var(--d-line); margin-right:10px; vertical-align:middle; text-align:center; line-height:16px; font-size:13px; color:var(--d-good); }
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-tick { background: var(--d-good); border-color: var(--d-good); color: var(--d-card); }
</style>
<script>
  var DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];
  var prs = DEFAULT;

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    prs.forEach(function (pr, i) {
      var li = document.createElement("li");
      li.className = "k-row";
      li.setAttribute("role", "button");
      li.setAttribute("aria-pressed", pr.done ? "true" : "false");
      li.innerHTML = '<span class="k-tick">' + (pr.done ? "✓" : "") + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-main"></span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-sub"></span>';
      li.querySelector(".k-row-main").textContent = pr.id + " · " + pr.title;
      li.querySelector(".k-row-sub").textContent = pr.waiting;
      li.addEventListener("click", function () { toggle(i); });
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    var open = prs.filter(function (p) { return !p.done; }).length;
    document.getElementById("count").textContent = String(open);
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = open === 0
      ? "all landed"
      : (prs.length - open) + " landed · tick each one as it lands";
    ducter.summary(open + " PRs waiting");
  }

  function save() { ducter.set("prs", prs); }

  function toggle(i) {
    prs[i].done = !prs[i].done;
    save();
    render();
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function (saved) {
    if (Array.isArray(saved) && saved.length) prs = saved;
    else { prs = DEFAULT; save(); }
    render();
  });

  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function (saved) {
      if (Array.isArray(saved)) { prs = saved; render(); }
    });
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter#412","title":"compaction cut","waiting":"Weizhen's review, since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem#10287","title":"retry on 502","waiting":"CI is failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb#1592","title":"flaky compaction test","waiting":"Alice's review, since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter#418","title":"apps bar","waiting":"still a draft","done":false}],"default":[{"id":"ducter#412","title":"compaction cut","waiting":"Weizhen's review, since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem#10287","title":"retry on 502","waiting":"CI is failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb#1592","title":"flaky compaction test","waiting":"Alice's review, since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter#418","title":"apps bar","waiting":"still a draft","done":false}]}

```

  $("save").textContent = "改好了"; $("del").hidden = false; $("addbox").open = true;

$("save").onclick = () => {
  const amt = $("amt").value;
  if (!amt) return;
  const it = { trip: $("trip").value.trim() || "出差", day: $("day").value.trim(), cat: cat, amt: Number(amt) };
  if (ed >= 0) xs[ed] = it; else xs.push(it);
  ducter.set("items", xs); reset(); draw();
};
$("del").onclick = () => {

reset(); draw();
